d[\"__row_order\"] = range(len(d))\n            d = d.sort_values([\"__parsed_event_time\", \"__row_order\"], ascending=[True, True])\n        else:\n            # No parseable timestamps; keep Needed\"]]\n        approval_needed = \"\" if is_blank(approval_needed_raw) else str(approval_needed_raw).strip()\n\n        key = (facility, session_id, bin_id)\n        has_final = key in final_lookup\n        count_qty = final_lookup.get(key)\n\n        missing_final = 0 if has_final else 1\n\n        approval_gap = 0\n        if missing_final == 0:\n            if approval_needed.strip().upper() == \"YES\":\n                if expected_qty is not None and allowed_var is not None and count_qty is not None:\n                    if abs(expected_qty - float(count_qty)) > allowed_var:\n                        approval_gap = 1\n\n        total_errors = int(missing_final + approval_gap)\n        err_summary = compute_error_summary(int(missing_final), int(approval_gap))\n\n        formatted_rows.append(\n            {\n                \"Facility\": facility,\n                \"Session ID\": session_id,\n                \"Bin ID\": bin_id,\n                \"Product ID\": to_yyyy_mm_dd_string(product_id),\n                \"Expected Qty\": expected_qty if expected_qty is not None else None,\n                \"Allowed Variance\": allowed_var if allowed_var is not None else None,\n                \"Approval Needed\": approval_needed,\n                \"Missing Final Count\": int(missing_final),\n                \"Approval Gap\": int(approval_gap),\n                \"Total Errors\": int(total_errors),\n                \"Error Summary\": err_summary,\n            }\n        )\n\n    df_formatted = pd.DataFrame(formatted_rows, columns=FORMATTED_HEADERS)\n\n    # Summary\n    grp = (\n        df_formatted.groupby([\"Facility\", \"Session ID\"], as_index=False)[\n            [\"Missing Final Count\", \"Approval Gap\", \"Total Errors\"]\n        ]\n        .sum()\n        .rename(\n            columns={\n                \"Missing Final Count\": \"Missing Final Counts\",\n                \"Approval Gap\": \"Approval Gaps\",\n            }\n        )\n    )\n\n    grp = grp[grp[\"Total Errors\"] > 0].copy()\n    grp = grp.sort_values([\"Facility\", \"Session ID\"], ascending=[True, True])\n\n    grand_total = {\n        \"Facility\": \"Grand Total\",\n        \"Session ID\": \"-\",\n        \"Missing Final Counts\": int(grp[\"Missing Final Counts\"].sum()) if len(grp) else 0,\n        \"Approval Gaps\": int(grp[\"Approval Gaps\"].sum()) if len(grp) else 0,\n        \"Total Errors\": int(grp[\"Total Errors\"].sum()) if len(grp) else 0,\n    }\n\n    df_summary = pd.concat([grp, pd.DataFrame([grand_total])], ignore_index=True)\n    df_summary = df_summary[SUMMARY_HEADERS]\n\n    # Build workbook\n    out_wb = openpyxl.Workbook()\n    # Remove default sheet\n    if out_wb.sheetnames:\n        out_wb.remove(out_wb[out_wb.sheetnames[0]])\n\n    # 1) Overview sheet copied exactly from template\n    tpl_wb = openpyxl.load_workbook(TEMPLATE_PATH)\n    if \"Overview\" not in tpl_wb.sheetnames:\n        tpl_wb.close()\n        raise ValueError(f\"Template workbook missing 'Overview' sheet. Found: {tpl_wb.sheetnames}\")\n    copy_worksheet_exact(tpl_wb[\"Overview\"], out_wb, title=\"Overview\")\n    tpl_wb.close()\n\n    # 2) RawData (copy plan table values)\n    ws_raw = out_wb.create_sheet(title=\"RawData\")\n\n    # headers\n    for c_idx, col_name in enumerate(df_plan.columns, start=1):\n        ws_raw.cell(row=1, column=c_idx, value=str(col_name))\n\n    # values (preserve row order)\n    for r_idx, row_vals in enumerate(df_plan.to_numpy().tolist(), start=2):\n        for c_idx, val in enumerate(row_vals, start=1):\n            ws_raw.cell(row=r_idx, column=c_idx, value=to_yyyy_mm_dd_string(val))\n\n    # 3) Formatted Data\n    ws_fmt = out_wb.create_sheet(title=\"Formatted Data\")\n    for c_idx, h in enumerate(FORMATTED_HEADERS, start=1):\n        ws_fmt.cell(row=1, column=c_idx, value=h)\n\n    for r_idx, row_vals in enumerate(df_formatted[FORMATTED_HEADERS].to_numpy().tolist(), start=2):\n        for c_idx, val in enumerate(row_vals, start=1):\n            val = to_yyyy_mm_dd_string(val)\n            ws_fmt.cell(row=r_idx, column=c_idx, value=val)\n\n    # 4) Summary\n    ws_sum = out_wb.create_sheet(title=\"Summary\")\n    for c_idx, h in enumerate(SUMMARY_HEADERS, start=1):\n        ws_sum.cell(row=1, column=c_idx, value=h)\n\n    for r_idx, row in enumerate(df_summary.itertuples(index=False), start=2):\n        for c_idx, h in enumerate(SUMMARY_HEADERS, start=1):\n            val = getattr(row, f\"_{c_idx}\")\n            val = to_yyyy_mm_dd_string(val)\n            ws_sum.cell(row=r_idx, column=c_idx, value=val)\n\n    return out_wb, df_formatted, df_summary\n\n\ndef create_word_brief(df_summary: pd.DataFrame) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create an executive summary Word document using computed totals.\"\"\"\n\n    #
